std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n            \n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    \n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        // Update all elements\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n            const ElementStatic& elem_static = world.elements_static[i];\n            const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[i];\n            const Material& mat = world.materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n            \n            // Start with external flow\n            val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n            \n            // Add flux from all connected elements\n            for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[neighbor_idx];\n                total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n            }\n            \n            // Update element state\n            ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];\n            elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n            elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n        }\n        \n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int n_elems_root = 512;\n    int n_iters = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_elems_root = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_iters = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n \n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    printf(\"Unstructured Mesh Energy Transfer Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"============================================\\n\");\n \"disabled\");\n    printf(\"\\n\");\n    \n    // Build the unstructured mesh\n    printf(\"Building unstructured mesh...\\n\");\n    World world;\n    buildSquare2D(world, n_elems_root);\n    \n    // Calculate memory usage\n    const size_t static_mem = world.elements_static.size() * sizeof(ElementStatic);\n    const size_t dynamic_mem = world.elements_dynamic.size() * sizeof(ElementDynamic) * 2;\n    const size_t total_mem = static_mem + dynamic_mem;\n    printf(\"Memory usage: %.2f MB (static: %.2f MB, dynamic: %.2f MB)\\n\",\n           total_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n           static_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n           dynamic_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));\n    printf(\"\\n\");\n    \n    // Run simulation\n    printf(\"Running simulation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    runSimulation(world, n_iters);\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration_ms = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start).count();\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration_ms);\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const int                                              \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                              \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__,    \\\n                    __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err_));                        \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                 \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements.\n// Explicit round-to-nearest intrinsics prevent FMA contraction so results are\n// bitwise identical to the sequential evaluation order:\n//   ((other - this) * transfer_coeff) * connection_flux * 0.25\n__device__ __forceinline__ val_t computeFlux(val_t transfer_coeff, val_t this_energy,\n                                             val_t connection_flux, val_t other_energy) {\n    val_t f = __dmul_rn(__dsub_rn(other_energy, this_energy), transfer_coeff);\n    f = __dmul_rn(f, connection_flux);\n    return __dmul_rn(f, 0.25);\n}\n\n// Device-side mesh in structure-of-arrays layout (coalesced access).\n// Connection arrays are stored slot-major: slot j of element i is at [j * n + i].\nstruct DeviceMesh {\n    size_t n;\n    const uint8_t* num_conn;        // [n]\n    const uint8_t* mat_idx;         // [n] (only used when n_materials <= 256)\n    const uint32_t* mat_idx32;      // [n] fallback for many materials\n    const uint32_t* conn_idx;       // [MAX_CONNECTIONS * n]\n    const val_t* conn_flux;         // [MAX_CONNECTIONS * n] (unused if UNIT_FLUX)\n    const Material* materials;      // [n_materials]\n};\n\n// One simulation step. Each thread updates one element.\n// UNIT_FLUX: all connection flux coefficients are exactly 1.0, so multiplying\n// by them is an exact identity and the loads can be skipped.\ntemplate <bool UNIT_FLUX, bool SMALL_MAT>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256)\nstepKernel(DeviceMesh mesh, const val_t* __restrict__ energy_in,\n           val_t* __restrict__ energy_out, val_t* __restrict__ total_flux_acc) {\n    const size_t n = mesh.n;\n    for (size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < n;\n         i += (size_t)gridDim.x * blockDim.x) {\n        const uint32_t m = SMALL_MAT ? (uint32_t)__ldg(&mesh.mat_idx[i]) : __ldg(&mesh.mat_idx32[i]);\n        const val_t transfer_coeff = __ldg(&mesh.materials[m].transfer_coeff);\n        const val_t external_flow = __ldg(&mesh.materials[m].external_flow);\n        const int nc = __ldg(&mesh.num_conn[i]);\n        const val_t e = __ldg(&energy_in[i]);\n\n        // Start with external flow\n        val_t total_flux = external_flow;\n\n        // Add flux from all connected elements (same order as sequential code)\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {\n            if (j < nc) {\n                const uint32_t nb = __ldg(&mesh.conn_idx[j * n + i]);\n                const val_t cf = UNIT_FLUX ? 1.0 : __ldg(&mesh.conn_flux[j * n + i]);\n                total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, computeFlux(transfer_coeff, e, cf, __ldg(&energy_in[nb])));\n            }\n        }\n\n        energy_out[i] = __dadd_rn(e, total_flux);\n        total_flux_acc[i] = __dadd_rn(total_flux_acc[i], fabs(total_flux));\n    }\n}\n\ntemplate <bool UNIT_FLUX, bool SMALL_MAT>\nstatic void launchSteps(const DeviceMesh& mesh, val_t* d_e0, val_t* d_e1, val_t* d_tf,\n                        int n_iters, cudaStream_t stream) {\n    constexpr int block = 256;\n    int dev = 0, n_sm = 0, blocks_per_sm = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&dev));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&n_sm, cudaDevAttrMultiProcessorCount, dev));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaOccupancyMaxActiveBlocksPerMultiprocessor(\n        &blocks_per_sm, stepKernel<UNIT_FLUX, Each thread updates one element.\n// UNIT_FLUX: all connection flux coefficients are exactly 1.0, so multiplying\n// by them is an exact identity and the loads can be skipped.\ntemplate <bool UNIT_FLUX, bool SMALL_MAT>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256)\nstepKernel(DeviceMesh mesh, const val_t* __restrict__ energy_in,\n           val_t* __restrict__ energy_out, val_t* __restrict__ total_flux_acc) {\n    const size_t n = mesh.n;\n    for (size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < n;\n         i += (size_t)gridDim.x * blockDim.x) {\n        const uint32_t m = SMALL_MAT ? (uint32_t)__ldg(&mesh.mat_idx[i]) : __ldg(&mesh.mat_idx32[i]);\n        const val_t transfer_coeff = __ldg(&mesh.materials[m].transfer_coeff);\n        const val_t external_flow = __ldg(&mesh.materials[m].external_flow);\n        const int nc = __ldg(&mesh.num_conn[i]);\n        const val_t e = __ldg(&energy_in[i]);\n\n        // Start with external flow\n        val_t total_flux = external_flow;\n\n        // Add flux from all connected elements (same order as sequential code)\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {\n            if (j < nc) {\n                const uint32_t nb = __ldg(&mesh.conn_idx[j * n + i]);\n                const val_t cf = UNIT_FLUX ? 1.0 : __ldg(&mesh.conn_flux[j * n + i]);\n                total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux, computeFlux(transfer_coeff, e, cf, __ldg(&energy_in[nb])));\n            }\n        }\n\n        energy_out[i] = __dadd_rn(e, total_flux);\n        total_flux_acc[i] = __dadd_rn(total_flux_acc[i], fabs(total_flux));\n    }\n}\n\ntemplate <bool UNIT_FLUX, bool SMALL_MAT>\nstatic void launchSteps(const DeviceMesh& mesh, val_t* d_e0, val_t* d_e1, val_t* d_tf,\n                        int n_iters, cudaStream_t stream) {\n    constexpr int block = 256;\n    int dev = 0, n_sm = 0, blocks_per_sm = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&dev));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&n_sm, cudaDevAttrMultiProcessorCount, dev));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaOccupancyMaxActiveBlocksPerMultiprocessor(\n        &blocks_per_sm, stepKernel<UNIT_FLUX, SMALL_MAT>, block, 0));\n    const size_t needed = (mesh.n + block - 1) / block;\n    const size_t max_resident = (size_t)n_sm * std::max(blocks_per_sm, 1) * 8;\n    const unsigned grid = (unsigned)std::max<size_t>(1, std::min(needed, max_resident));\n\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        stepKernel<UNIT_FLUX, SMALL_MAT><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(mesh, d_e0, d_e1, d_tf);\n        std::swap(d_e0, d_e1);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    if (n_elems == 0) return;\n    const size_t n_mats = world.materials.size();\n    const bool small_mat = n_mats <= 256;\n\n    // Convert mesh to device SoA layout\n    std::vector<uint8_t> h_num_conn(n_elems);\n    std::vector<uint8_t> h_mat8(small_mat ? n_elems : 0);\n    std::vector<uint32_t> h_mat32(small_mat ? 0 : n_elems);\n    std::vector<uint32_t> h_conn_idx(MAX_CONNECTIONS * n_elems, 0);\n    std::vector<val_t> h_conn_flux;\n    std::vector<val_t> h_e(n_elems), h_tf(n_elems);\n\n    bool unit_flux = true;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        const ElementStatic& s = world.elements_static[i];\n        for (idx_t j = 0; j < s.num_connections; ++j)\n            if (s.connected_flux[j] != 1.0) unit_flux = false;\n    }\n    if (!unit_flux) h_conn_flux.assign(MAX_CONNECTIONS * n_elems, 0.0);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        const ElementStatic& s = world.elements_static[i];\n        h_num_conn[i] = (uint8_t)s.num_connections;\n        if (small_mat) h_mat8[i] = (uint8_t)s.material_idx;\n        else h_mat32[i] = (uint32_t)s.material_idx;\n        for (idx_t j = 0; j < s.num_connections; ++j) {\n            h_conn_idx[j * n_elems + i] = (uint32_t)s.connected_idx[j];\n            if (!unit_flux) h_conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] = s.connected_flux[j];\n        }\n        h_e[i] = world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy;\n        h_tf[i] = world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux;\n    }\n\n    // Allocate and upload\n    uint8_t *d_num_conn = nullptr, *d_mat8 = nullptr;\n    uint32_t *d_mat32 = nullptr, *d_conn_idx = nullptr;\n    val_t *d_conn_flux = nullptr, *d_e0 = nullptr, *d_e1 = nullptr, *d_tf = nullptr;\n    Material* d_mats = nullptr;\n    cudaStream_t stream;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&stream));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_num_conn, n_elems));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_num_conn, h_num_conn.data(), n_elems, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n    if (small_mat) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_mat8, n_elems));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_mat8, h_mat8.data(), n_elems, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n    } else {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_mat32, n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_mat32, h_mat32.data(), n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_conn_idx, h_conn_idx.size() * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_conn_idx, h_conn_idx.data(), h_conn_idx.size() * sizeof(uint32_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n    if (!unit_flux) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_conn_flux, h_conn_flux.size() * sizeof(val_t)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_conn_flux, h_conn_flux.data(), h_conn_flux.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_mats, std::max<size_t>(n_mats, 1) * sizeof(Material)));\n    if (n_mats) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_mats, world.materials.data(), n_mats * sizeof(Material), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_e0, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_e1, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_tf, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_e0, h_e.data(), n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_tf, h_tf.data(), n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n\n    DeviceMesh mesh{n_elems, d_num_conn, d_mat8, d_mat32, d_conn_idx, d_conn_flux, d_mats};\n\n    if (unit_flux) {\n        if (small_mat) launchSteps<true, true>(mesh, d_e0, d_e1, d_tf, n_iters, stream);\n        else launchSteps<true, false>(mesh, d_e0, d_e1, d_tf, n_iters, stream);\n    } else {\n        if (small_mat) launchSteps<false, true>(mesh, d_e0, d_e1, d_tf, n_iters, stream);\n        else launchSteps<false, false>(mesh, d_e0, d_e1, d_tf, n_iters, stream);\n    }\n\n    // Final state lives in d_e0 after an even number of steps, else d_e1\n    const val_t* d_result = (n_iters > 0 && (n_iters & 1)) ? d_e1 : d_e0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_e.data(), d_result, n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_tf.data(), d_tf, n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = h_e[i];\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = h_tf[i];\n    }\n\n    cudaFree(d_num_conn); cudaFree(d_mat8); cudaFree(d_mat32); cudaFree(d_conn_idx);\n    cudaFree(d_conn_flux); cudaFree(d_mats); cudaFree(d_e0); cudaFree(d_e1); cudaFree(d_tf);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));\n}\n\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new+src[end:]\nsrc=src.replace('#include <vector>\\n','#include <vector>\\n\\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\\n',1)\nsrc=src.replace(''' Each thread updates one element.\n// UNIT_FLUX: all connection flux coefficients are exactly 1.0, so multiplying\n// by them is an exact identity and the loads can be skipped.\ntemplate <bool UNIT_FLUX, typename MatT>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256)\nstepKernel(DeviceMesh<MatT> mesh, const val_t* __restrict__ energy_in,\n           val_t* __restrict__ energy_out, val_t* __restrict__ total_flux_acc) {\n    const size_t n = mesh.n;\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n\n    const uint32_t m = __ldg(&mesh.mat_idx[i]);\n    const int nc = __ldg(&mesh.num_conn[i]);\n    const val_t e = __ldg(&energy_in[i]);\n    const val_t tf_old = __ldg(&total_flux_acc[i]);\n    const val_t transfer_coeff = __ldg(&mesh.materials[m].transfer_coeff);\n\n    // Start with external flow\n    val_t total_flux = __ldg(&mesh.materials[m].external_flow);\n\n    // Add flux from all connected elements (same order as sequential code)\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ ci = mesh.conn_idx + i;\n    const val_t* __restrict__ cf = UNIT_FLUX ? nullptr : mesh.conn_flux + i;\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int j = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&buf_[b], CHUNK_BYTES));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&ev_[b], cudaEventDisableTiming));\n        }\n    }\n    ~Stager() {\n        for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n            cudaEventSynchronize(ev_[b]);\n            cudaFreeHost(buf_[b]);\n            cudaEventDestroy(ev_[b]);\n        }\n    }\n    // Number of elements per chunk given the per-element staged byte count\n    size_t chunkElems(size_t bytes_per_elem) const { return CHUNK_BYTES / bytes_per_elem; k < cnt; ++k) {\n                const ElementStatic& s = world.elements_static[base + k];\n                const ElementDynamic& d = world.elements_dynamic[base + k];\n                const idx_t nc = s.num_connections;\n                h_nc[k] = (uint8_t)nc;\n                h_mat[k] = (MatT)s.material_idx;\n                h_e[k] = d.current_energy;\n                h_tf[k] = d.total_flux;\n                max_nc = std::max(max_nc, nc);\n                for (idx_t j = 0; j < max_nc; ++j) h_idx[j * C + k] = 0;\n            }\n            // Fill earlier elements' unused slots up to the chunk's max (defined data)\n            stager.h2d(d_num_conn + base, 0, cnt);\n            stager.h2d(d_mat + base, C, cnt * sizeof(MatT));\n            stager.h2d(d_e0 + base, C * (1 + sizeof(MatT)), cnt * sizeof(val_t));\n            stager.h2d(d_tf + base, C * (1 + sizeof(MatT)) + C * sizeof(val_t), cnt * sizeof(val_t));\n            const size_t idx_off = reinterpret_cast<char*>(h_idx) - hb;\n            for (idx_t j = 0; Each thread updates one element.\n// UNIT_FLUX: all connection flux coefficients are exactly 1.0, so multiplying\n// by them is an exact identity and the loads can be skipped.\ntemplate <bool UNIT_FLUX, typename MatT>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256)\nstepKernel(DeviceMesh<MatT> mesh, const val_t* __restrict__ energy_in,\n           val_t* __restrict__ energy_out, val_t* __restrict__ total_flux_acc) {\n    const size_t n = mesh.n;\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n\n    const uint32_t m = __ldg(&mesh.mat_idx[i]);\n    const int nc = __ldg(&mesh.num_conn[i]);\n    const val_t e = __ldg(&energy_in[i]);\n    const val_t tf_old = __ldg(&total_flux_acc[i]);\n    const val_t transfer_coeff = __ldg(&mesh.materials[m].transfer_coeff);\n\n    // Start with external flow\n    val_t total_flux = __ldg(&mesh.materials[m].external_flow);\n\n    // Add flux from all connected elements (same order as sequential code)\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ ci = mesh.conn_idx + i;\n    const val_t* __restrict__ cf = UNIT_FLUX ? nullptr : mesh.conn_flux + i;\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {\n        if (j < nc) {\n            const uint32_t nb = __ldg(ci + j * n);\n            const val_t flux_coeff = UNIT_FLUX ? 1.0 : __ldg(cf + j * n);\n            total_flux = __dadd_rn(total_flux,\n                                   computeFlux(transfer_coeff, e, flux_coeff, __ldg(&energy_in[nb])));\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Update element state\n    energy_out[i] = __dadd_rn(e, total_flux);\n    total_flux_acc[i] = __dadd_rn(tf_old, fabs(total_flux));\n}\n\ntemplate <bool UNIT_FLUX, typename MatT>\nstatic void launchSteps(const DeviceMesh<MatT>& mesh, val_t* d_e0, val_t* d_e1, val_t* d_tf,\n                        int n_iters, cudaStream_t stream) {\n    constexpr int block = 256;\n    const unsigned grid = (unsigned)((mesh.n + block - 1) / block);\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        stepKernel<UNIT_FLUX, MatT><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(mesh, d_e0, d_e1, d_tf);\n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(d_e0, d_e1);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n}\n\n// Pipelined host<->device staging through double-buffered pinned memory, so\n// host-side (un)packing overlaps with PCIe transfers.\nclass Stager {\npublic:\n    static constexpr size_t CHUNK_BYTES = size_t(32) << 20;\n    explicit Stager(cudaStream_t s) : stream_(s) {\n        for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&buf_[b], CHUNK_BYTES));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&ev_[b], cudaEventDisableTiming));\n        }\n    }\n    ~Stager() {\n        for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n            cudaEventSynchronize(ev_[b]);\n            cudaFreeHost(buf_[b]);\n            cudaEventDestroy(ev_[b]);\n        }\n    }\n    // Number of elements per chunk given the per-element staged byte count\n    size_t chunkElems(size_t bytes_per_elem) const { return CHUNK_BYTES / bytes_per_elem; }\n\n    // Get a free host buffer (waits until its previous transfer completed)\n    char* acquire() {\n        cur_ ^= 1;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(ev_[cur_]));\n        return buf_[cur_];\n    }\n    void h2d(void* dst, size_t offset_in_buf, size_t bytes) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dst, buf_[cur_] + offset_in_buf, bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream_));\n    }\n    void d2h(size_t offset_in_buf, const void* src, size_t bytes) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(buf_[cur_] + offset_in_buf, src, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream_));\n    }\n    void release() { CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(ev_[cur_], stream_)); }\n    void waitCurrent() { CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(ev_[cur_])); }\n\nprivate:\n    cudaStream_t stream_;\n    char* buf_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    cudaEvent_t ev_[2];\n    int cur_ = 1;\n};\n\ntemplate <typename MatT>\nstatic void simulateOnDevice(World& world, const int n_iters, cudaStream_t stream, Stager& stager) {\n    const size_t n = world.elements_static.size();\n    const size_t n_mats = world.materials.size();\n\n    uint8_t* d_num_conn = nullptr;\n    MatT* d_mat = nullptr;\n    uint32_t* d_conn_idx = nullptr;\n    val_t *d_conn_flux = nullptr, *d_e0 = nullptr, *d_e1 = nullptr, *d_tf = nullptr;\n    Material* d_mats = nullptr;\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_num_conn, n));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_mat, n * sizeof(MatT)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_conn_idx, MAX_CONNECTIONS * n * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_e0, n * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_e1, n * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_tf, n * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_mats, std::max<size_t>(n_mats, 1) * sizeof(Material)));\n    if (n_mats) {\n        char* hb = stager.acquire();\n        memcpy(hb, world.materials.data(), n_mats * sizeof(Material));\n        stager.h2d(d_mats, 0, n_mats * sizeof(Material));\n        stager.release();\n    }\n\n    // Pass 1: connectivity, materials and initial dynamic state.\n    // Chunk layout: [num_conn C][mat C][energy C][total_flux C][idx slot 0 C]...[idx slot 7 C]\n    bool unit_flux = true;\n    {\n        const size_t bpe = 1 + sizeof(MatT) + 2 * sizeof(val_t) + MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(uint32_t);\n        const size_t C = stager.chunkElems(bpe);\n        for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += C) {\n            const size_t cnt = std::min(C, n - base);\n            char* hb = stager.acquire();\n            uint8_t* h_nc = reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(hb);\n            MatT* h_mat = reinterpret_cast<MatT*>(hb + C);\n            val_t* h_e = reinterpret_cast<val_t*>(hb + C * (1 + sizeof(MatT)));\n            val_t* h_tf = h_e + C;\n            uint32_t* h_idx = reinterpret_cast<uint32_t*>(h_tf + C);\n            idx_t max_nc = 0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < cnt; ++k) {\n                const ElementStatic& s = world.elements_static[base + k];\n                const ElementDynamic& d = world.elements_dynamic[base + k];\n                const idx_t nc = s.num_connections;\n                h_nc[k] = (uint8_t)nc;\n                h_mat[k] = (MatT)s.material_idx;\n                h_e[k] = d.current_energy;\n                h_tf[k] = d.total_flux;\n                max_nc = std::max(max_nc, nc);\n                for (idx_t j = 0; j < max_nc; ++j) h_idx[j * C + k] = 0;\n            }\n            // Fill earlier elements' unused slots up to the chunk's max (defined data)\n            stager.h2d(d_num_conn + base, 0, cnt);\n            stager.h2d(d_mat + base, C, cnt * sizeof(MatT));\n            stager.h2d(d_e0 + base, C * (1 + sizeof(MatT)), cnt * sizeof(val_t));\n            stager.h2d(d_tf + base, C * (1 + sizeof(MatT)) + C * sizeof(val_t), cnt * sizeof(val_t));\n            const size_t idx_off = reinterpret_cast<char*>(h_idx) - hb;\n            for (idx_t j = 0; j < max_nc; ++j)\n                stager.h2d(d_conn_idx + j * n + base, idx_off + j * C * sizeof(uint32_t),\n                           cnt * sizeof(uint32_t));\n            stager.release();\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Pass 2 (only if needed): non-unit connection flux coefficients.\n    if (!unit_flux) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_conn_flux, MAX_CONNECTIONS * n * sizeof(val_t)));\n        const size_t C = stager.chunkElems(MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t));\n        for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += C) {\n            const size_t cnt = std::min(C, n - base);\n            val_t* h_f = reinterpret_cast<val_t*>(stager.acquire());\n            idx_t max_nc = 0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < cnt; ++k) {\n                const ElementStatic& s = world.elements_static[base + k];\n                max_nc = std::max(max_nc, s.num_connections);\n                for (idx_t j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j)\n                    h_f[j * C + k] = j < s.num_connections ? s.connected_flux[j] : 0.0;\n            }\n            for (idx_t j = 0; j < max_nc; ++j)\n                stager.h2d(d_conn_flux + j * n + base, j * C * sizeof(val_t), cnt * sizeof(val_t));\n            stager.release();\n        }\n    }\n\n    DeviceMesh<MatT> mesh{n, d_num_conn, d_mat, d_conn_idx, d_conn_flux, d_mats};\n    if (unit_flux) launchSteps<true, MatT>(mesh, d_e0, d_e1, d_tf, n_iters, stream);\n    else launchSteps<false, MatT>(mesh, d_e0, d_e1, d_tf, n_iters, stream);\n\n    // Final state lives in d_e0 after an even number of steps, else d_e1\n    const val_t* d_result = (n_iters > 0 && (n_iters & 1)) ? d_e1 : d_e0;\n\n    // Download results, unpacking each chunk while the next one transfers\n    {\n        const size_t C = stager.chunkElems(2 * sizeof(val_t));\n        size_t prev_base = 0, prev_cnt = 0;\n        val_t* prev_buf = nullptr;\n        auto unpack = [&](const val_t* hb, size_t base, size_t cnt) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < cnt; ++k) {\n                world.elements_dynamic[base + k].current_energy = hb[k];\n                world.elements_dynamic[base + k].total_flux = hb[C + k];\n            }\n        };\n        for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += C) {\n            const size_t cnt = std::min(C, n - base);\n            val_t* hb = reinterpret_cast<val_t*>(stager.acquire());\n            stager.d2h(0, d_result + base, cnt * sizeof(val_t));\n            stager.d2h(C * sizeof(val_t), d_tf + base, cnt * sizeof(val_t));\n            stager.release();\n            if (prev_buf) unpack(prev_buf, prev_base, prev_cnt);\n            prev_buf = nullptr;\n            // Wait for this chunk; the unpack of it overlaps the next transfer\n            stager.waitCurrent();\n            prev_buf = hb; prev_base = base; prev_cnt = cnt;\n        }\n        if (prev_buf) unpack(prev_buf, prev_base, prev_cnt);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));\n\n    cudaFree(d_num_conn); cudaFree(d_mat); cudaFree(d_conn_idx); cudaFree(d_conn_flux);\n    cudaFree(d_mats); cudaFree(d_e0); cudaFree(d_e1); cudaFree(d_tf);\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    if (world.elements_static.empty()) return;\n    cudaStream_t stream;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));\n    {\n        Stager stager(stream);\n        if (world.materials.size() <= 256) simulateOnDevice<uint8_t>(world, n_iters, stream, stager);\n        else simulateOnDevice<uint32_t>(world, n_iters, stream, stager);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));\n}\n\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new+src[end:]\nopen('unstructured.cpp','w').write(src)\nEOF\ncmake --build build 2>&1 | grep -E \"error|warning\" ; Its output is bit-identical to the original program in every case I tested, and large runs are 40–100× faster. I only changed `unstructured/unstructured.cpp` and `CMakeLists.txt`, and the executable is still `unstructured`.\n\n**Results** (RTX 3090 vs. the original single-threaded CPU build):\n\n| Run (grid, iterations) | Original | CUDA | Hash matches |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 4096², 200 | 31,061 ms | 452 ms | ✓ |\n| 2000², 1000 | ~37 s | 326 ms | ✓ |\n| 512², 5000 | — | 128 ms | ✓ |\n| 512², 10 (default) | 22 ms | 16 ms | ✓ |\n\n- **Kernel speed:** one step on the 4096² grid takes about 1.0 ms on the GPU, versus 156 ms on the CPU. That is close to the card's memory-bandwidth limit.\n- **What the timer includes:** copying the mesh to the GPU and the results back is inside the timed section, as it should be. On the 4096² grid that copying is about 250 ms of the 452 ms, and it is also most of the 16 ms for the small default run. CUDA's one-time startup is done before the timer starts.\n\n**How it works**\n- **Kernel:** one GPU thread updates one element each step, reading the two energy buffers alternately, as the original does.\n- **Data layout:** the mesh is converted to a compact column-per-field layout for the GPU (8-bit connection counts, 32-bit neighbour indices).\n- **Copying:** data goes through small 2 MB pinned buffers, so packing on the CPU overlaps with the transfer. Ordinary memory copied at only ~3.7 GB/s on this machine, and allocating large pinned buffers took about 1.5 ms per MB.\n- **Fewer operations:** this card is slow at double-precision maths, which was the real bottleneck. If every connection coefficient is exactly 1.0, the kernel skips that multiply. It also replaces `(d·tc)·0.25` with `d·(tc·0.25)`, but only when the result is guaranteed to be the same bits. It checks this with integer operations and otherwise falls back to the original formula.\n\n**One subtlety you should know about:** in long runs (2000² for 1000 steps), the first GPU version disagreed with the original's hash. The cause was the original build, not the GPU code. GCC (with `-march=native`) handles the last connection of an element with an odd number of connections using a fused multiply-add, and all other connections without one. This only changes results when values get extremely small (subnormal), which happens at the edge of the spreading energy after a few hundred steps. I copied that exact behaviour into the kernel, so it now matches the reference build bit-for-bit. That includes stress tests with non-unit coefficients, subnormal values, negative coefficients, and overflow to inf/NaN. The catch: this matches the reference compiled with this machine's GCC 13.2 and the original flags. If the reference is built with strict floating-point (`-ffp-contract=off`), results can differ only in those subnormal edge cases.\n\n**Build changes:** CMake now builds `unstructured.cpp` as CUDA, targets the GPU in the machine (`native` architecture), defaults to a Release build, and requires CMake 3.18 or newer.
